⏺ Done. The Leistungen background is now a single stacked background with the
  exact layer order:

  1. Gradient (rgba(13,7,3,0) → #0D0703) — normal, blends the top into the
  section above
  2. 30% black — multiply over the image
  3. The image
  4. #000 base

  So the 30% black now multiplies against the photo (richer, deeper darkening)
  instead of a flat alpha overlay. Builds clean — reload http://localhost:4321
  to check the depth.

❯ now go through the page and do a ux test                                      

⏺ I'll do a thorough UX audit. Since headless screenshots are unreliable here,
  I'll audit the code + interactions and flag anything that needs your eyes. Let
  me run a few checks first:

  Ran 2 shell commands (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ I've gone through the whole page (structure, interactions, flow, mobile
  behavior, links, accessibility). Here's the UX test report, prioritized. I
  can't reliably screenshot from here, so a few visual items are flagged for you
  to confirm — but most of these are verifiable in the code/flow.

  🔴 Critical — conversion path is a dead end

  - "Jetzt anfragen" leads nowhere. Every primary CTA (hero, Benefits, FAQ,
  service cards) links to /#kontakt, which is the CTA band — and that band's own
  "Jetzt anfragen" also links to /#kontakt (itself). There's no contact form, 
  booking, or mailto anywhere. The only working conversion is the tel: phone
  links. For a chauffeur service, the main "inquire" action must reach a form or
  at least a mailto:. This is the single biggest issue.
  - No persistent navigation. The nav is position: absolute inside the hero, so
  it scrolls away and never comes back. On mobile, once you scroll past the hero
  there's no way to open the menu or reach a CTA. Needs a sticky/condensed
  header on scroll.

  🟠 High — broken links & mismatches

  - 404 links (only index.astro exists): the vehicle "Fahrzeug ansehen"
  (/fahrzeuge/e-klasse…), and footer Impressum / Datenschutz / AGB / 
  Flughafentransfer. Impressum & Datenschutz are legally required in AT/EU —
  those pages must exist.
  - "Preise" nav → "Warum Vienna Grand" section (#preise sits on the benefits
  block). Clicking Preise scrolls to a non-pricing section. Rename, repoint, or
  add a pricing block.
  - Images are ~35 MB total (noise-texture.jpg alone is 8.9 MB, hero/cards ~3 MB
  each). On mobile this is a very slow first load — real UX cost. (Flagged
  before for the optimization phase.)

  🟡 Medium

  - "DE" language switch does nothing (no EN version yet) — wire it up or hide
  it until EN exists.
  - "Kontakt" nav → CTA band, but the actual address/email live in the footer —
  consider pointing Kontakt to the footer or a real contact section.
  - Flip cards (Über uns) only flip on mouse hover — no flip on touch or
  keyboard. It's tolerable because the front already shows the label + caption,
  but the interaction is invisible to mobile users.

  🟢 Low / polish

  - Fleet slider arrows are 42px (just under the 44px recommended touch target).
  - Flip-card hover transition isn't disabled under prefers-reduced-motion.
  - Muted body text #0c0a089e (62% on cream) — likely passes AA but worth
  confirming on the 14.5px feature descriptions.

  ---
  Quick wins I can do right now (no new pages needed):
  1. Make the nav sticky on scroll (condensed bar) so navigation/CTA is always
  reachable.
  2. Point all "Jetzt anfragen" CTAs to a mailto: (or a proper #kontakt form) so
  they actually do something.
  3. Fix "Preise" (repoint or rename) and disable/soften the not-yet-built links
  so they don't 404.

  Want me to knock out those three now, or would you like to pick a different
  subset? (Building the Impressum/Datenschutz/vehicle subpages is a larger,
  separate task whenever you're ready.)

      237 +<script>                                                      
      238 +  // Galerie — scroll-snap carousel with arrows, counter and p
          +rogress bar.                                                  
      239 +  const track = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-gal
          +-track]');                                                    
      240 +  if (track) {                                                
      241 +    const slides = [...track.children] as HTMLElement[];      
      242 +    const count = document.querySelector('[data-gal-count]'); 
      243 +    const bar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-gal
          +-bar]');                                                      
      244 +    const step = () => (slides[1] ? slides[1].offsetLeft - sli
          +des[0].offsetLeft : track.clientWidth);                       
      245 +    const update = () => {                                    
      246 +      const i = Math.min(slides.length, Math.round(track.scrol
          +lLeft / step()) + 1);                                         
      247 +      if (count) count.textContent = String(i).padStart(2, '0'
          +) + ' / ' + String(slides.length).padStart(2, '0');           
      248 +      if (bar) bar.style.inlineSize = Math.min(100, ((track.sc
          +rollLeft + track.clientWidth) / track.scrollWidth) * 100) + '%
          +';                                                            
      249 +    };                                                        
      250 +    document.querySelector('[data-gal-prev]')?.addEventListene
          +r('click', () => track.scrollBy({ left: -step(), behavior: 'sm
          +ooth' }));                                                    
      251 +    document.querySelector('[data-gal-next]')?.addEventListene
          +r('click', () => track.scrollBy({ left: step(), behavior: 'smo
          +oth' }));                                                     
      252 +    track.addEventListener('scroll', update, { passive: true }
          +);                                                            
      253 +    update();                                                 
      254 +  }

  which links to itself). No contact form or mailto:. This is the biggest gap —
  the site can't convert except by phone.
  2. 404 links:
    - Footer Impressum / Datenschutz / AGB — legally required in AT/EU, don't
  exist yet.
    - Flotte cards link to /fahrzeuge/s-klasse & /fahrzeuge/v-klasse — only
  e-klasse exists.
    - Services carousel cards link to 8 /leistungen/[slug] pages — only
  flughafen-transfer-wien exists.
  3. "DE" language toggle does nothing (no EN yet).
  4. "Preise" / "Kontakt" nav items still point at homepage anchors that aren't
  really pricing/contact.

  🟡 Remaining — polish/perf

  5. Image optimization — most Figma PNGs are 2–3 MB each (dozens of MB total).
  AVIF/WebP + resizing is the #1 performance win, especially mobile.
  6. Homepage de-dup — Fleet + Services-carousel exist both inline on the
  homepage and as components; I can swap the homepage to the shared ones.
  7. Visual QA — I've been building off your screenshots rather than seeing
  renders myself, so a pass through all three pages (desktop + mobile) would
  likely surface small tweaks.

  My suggested next step: wire the CTAs to a real destination (a /kontakt page
  with a form, or at minimum mailto:), and stub the legal pages — that makes the
  site actually launch-ready. Want me to start there, build the other
  vehicle/service pages, or do the image-optimization pass? Your call.

  Here's what this pass did, in the spirit of the design system (tighten
  consistency, don't redesign a strong identity):

  Consistency

  - Unified section rhythm — the vertical padding had drifted across 8 different
  clamp() values. Introduced a single --section-py token and applied it to 13 
  content sections (homepage, Services, Vehicles, Faq, CtaBand, Footer). Every
  section now breathes on the same scale. Intentional exceptions kept: the hero
  (tall top padding), the marquee, and the compact values strip.
  - Verified the identity was already tight — eyebrows (24px desktop / 1.05rem
  mobile), the 60×1.429px terracotta divider line, the muted body color
  (#0c0a089e), and pill radii are all uniform across pages.

  Responsiveness

  - overflow-x: clip safety net on body — no section can ever trigger a
  horizontal scrollbar on mobile (using clip not hidden, so position: fixed and
  scroll-anchoring stay intact).
  - Confirmed there are no fixed-width overflow risks anywhere.
  - Earlier this turn: fixed the fleet bar's cramped medium-width layout in the
  shared component (raised to a 1024px breakpoint so the clean stacked grid
  covers that range).

  Builds clean (3 pages). The single-source-of-truth components mean these stay
  consistent going forward.

  jobs):
  1. Image optimization — the Figma PNGs are 2–3 MB each; the

⏺ Now the vanilla elastic-bounce script (no GSAP needed). Let me add it at the
  end of the component:

⏺ Update(src/components/Footer.astro)
  ⎿  Added 65 lines
      146      .footer__col:not(:last-child) { border-block-end: 1px soli
           d rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1); padding-block-end: 2rem; }
      147    }
      148  </style>
      149 +                                                              
      150 +<script>                                                      
      151 +  // Footer top edge: sags at rest, springs flat with an elast
          +ic bounce when it                                             
      152 +  // scrolls into view. Bounce amplitude scales with scroll ve
          +locity.                                                       
      153 +  // Vanilla reimplementation of the GSAP MorphSVG demo — no d
          +ependencies.                                                  
      154 +  const footer = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-fo
          +oter]');                                                      
      155 +  const fill = footer?.querySelector<SVGPathElement>('[data-wa
          +ve-fill]');                                                   
      156 +  const edge = footer?.querySelector<SVGPathElement>('[data-wa
          +ve-edge]');                                                   
      157 +                                                              
      158 +  if (footer && fill && edge) {                               
      159 +    const SAG = 130;   // initial/off-screen sag (viewBox unit
          +s)                                                            
      160 +    const REST = 10;   // settled resting curve (near-flat)   
      161 +    const reduce = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce
          +)').matches;                                                  
      162 +                                                              
      163 +    const build = (m: number) => {                            
      164 +      const top = `M0,0 C0,0,464,${m.toFixed(1)},1139,${m.toFi
          +xed(1)} S2278,0,2278,0`;                                      
      165 +      fill.setAttribute('d', `${top} V683 H0 V0 Z`);          
      166 +      edge.setAttribute('d', top);                            
      167 +    };                                                        
      168 +                                                              
      169 +    if (reduce) {                                             
      170 +      build(REST);                                            
      171 +    } else {                                                  
      172 +      build(SAG);                                             
      173 +                                                              
      174 +      // Track scroll velocity (px per ~frame).               
      175 +      let lastY = window.scrollY, lastT = performance.now(), v
          +el = 0;                                                       
      176 +      window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {               
      177 +        const now = performance.now(), y = window.scrollY;    
      178 +        vel = ((y - lastY) / Math.max(now - lastT, 1)) * 16;  
      179 +        lastY = y; lastT = now;                               
      180 +      }, { passive: true });                                  
      181 +                                                              
      182 +      // elastic.out(amplitude, period)                       
      183 +      const elasticOut = (t: number, amp: number, period: numb
          +er) => {                                                      
      184 +        if (t <= 0) return 0;                                 
      185 +        if (t >= 1) return 1;                                 
      186 +        const s = (period / (2 * Math.PI)) * Math.asin(1 / Mat
          +h.max(amp, 1));                                               
      187 +        return amp * Math.pow(2, -10 * t) * Math.sin(((t - s) 
          +* (2 * Math.PI)) / period) + 1;                               
      188 +      };                                                      
      189 +                                                              
      190 +      const DUR = 1700;                                       
      191 +      let raf = 0, start = 0, amp = 1, period = 0.35;         
      192 +      const tick = (now: number) => {                         
      193 +        const t = Math.min((now - start) / DUR, 1);           
      194 +        const e = elasticOut(t, amp, period);                 
      195 +        build(SAG + (REST - SAG) * e);                        
      196 +        if (t < 1) raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);         
      197 +      };                                                      
      198 +      const play = () => {                                    
      199 +        const variation = Math.min(Math.abs(vel) / 60, 0.6);  
      200 +        amp = 1 + variation;                                  
      201 +        period = 0.35 - variation * 0.14;                     
      202 +        start = performance.now();                            
      203 +        cancelAnimationFrame(raf);                            
      204 +        raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);                    
      205 +      };                                                      
      206 +                                                              
      207 +      const io = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {      
      208 +        entries.forEach((en) => { if (en.isIntersecting) play(
          +); });                                                        
      209 +      }, { threshold: 0, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });  
      210 +      io.observe(footer);                                     
      211 +    }                                                         
      212 +  }                                                           
      213 +</script>                                                     

  Ran 1 shell command (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ Built and in the output. Here's what I made:

  The effect (from the pen "Footer Bounce Based on Scroll Speed"): the footer's
  top edge starts as a sagged curve, then springs flat with an elastic wobble
  the moment it scrolls into view — and the harder/faster you scroll into it,
  the bouncier the spring.

  How I built it — the original uses GSAP's MorphSVGPlugin, which is a paid Club
  GreenSock plugin. But since the two shapes are structurally identical (only
  the middle control point moves from 130 → 0), I reproduced it in plain vanilla
  JS — no GSAP, no paid plugin, no new dependencies. It tracks scroll velocity,
  then runs an elastic.out easing whose amplitude/period scale with that
  velocity, rebuilding the SVG path each frame.

  Adapted to our brand: since the footer sits on a dark section (dark-on-dark
  would be invisible), I gave the curve a terracotta edge line so the bounce is
  clearly visible, backed by the footer's dark fill so the top physically
  curves. It respects prefers-reduced-motion (settles to a static gentle curve,
  no animation).

  A couple of things worth knowing before you judge it:
  - It's subtle by design right now (thin terracotta line, gentle sag). Easy to
  dial up — thicker line, deeper sag, more bounce, or a filled terracotta
  gradient wave instead of just a line.
  - Because the section above the footer (the CTA band) is also dark, the fill
  curve barely reads — the terracotta line is what you'll see moving.

  Reload and scroll down to the footer to see it spring. Want me to make it more
  dramatic (deeper curve / bolder color / filled wave), keep it subtle, or try
  it in a different spot?

✻ Cogitated for 9m 22s

  logic to the nav based on the current path:

⏺ Update(src/components/Nav.astro)
  ⎿  Added 13 lines
       1  ---
       2  import { NAV_LINKS, CONTACT } from '../lib/site';
       3  const phoneHref = `tel:${CONTACT.phone.tel}`;
       4 +                                                               
       5 +// Highlight the current page. `path` is normalised without a t
         +railing slash.                                                 
       6 +const path = Astro.url.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';    
       7 +// A top-level item is active when the current page is inside i
         +ts section                                                     
       8 +// (e.g. any /leistungen/* page lights up "Leistungen"). Hash l
         +inks never match.                                              
       9 +const sectionActive = (href: string) => {                      
      10 +  if (href === '/') return path === '/';                       
      11 +  const seg = href.split('/')[1] ?? '';                        
      12 +  if (!seg || seg.startsWith('#')) return false;               
      13 +  return path === `/${seg}` || path.startsWith(`/${seg}/`);    
      14 +};                                                             
      15 +// A dropdown/sub link is active only on its exact page.       
      16 +const linkActive = (href: string) => path === href.replace(/\/+
         +$/, '');                                                       
      17  ---
      18  <header class="nav" data-nav>
      19    <nav class="nav__links" aria-label="Hauptnavigation">

⏺ Active states are live and verified across every page type:

  ┌──────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │           On page            │                Highlighted                │
  ├──────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Homepage                     │ Home                                      │
  ├──────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ /leistungen/chauffeurservice │ Leistungen (section) + Chauffeurservice   │
  │                              │ in the dropdown (exact page)              │
  ├──────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ /fahrzeuge/e-klasse          │ Fahrzeuge                                 │
  └──────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────┘

  How it looks:
  - Desktop top-level active item → white text with a persistent terracotta
  underline (not just on hover).
  - Desktop dropdown → the current service gets a terracotta-tinted background
  pill.
  - Mobile → active link + sub-link turn terracotta (sub-link also gets the
  terracotta accent border).

  Logic:
  - Top-level items light up by section — any /leistungen/* page activates
  "Leistungen", any /fahrzeuge/* activates "Fahrzeuge".
  - Dropdown/sub-links light up only on their exact page.
  - Hash links (Preise, Kontakt) are intentionally never marked active — they're
  homepage anchors, so there's no reliable page to match without scroll-spy.
  - Added aria-current for screen readers.

  It's all driven by Astro.url.pathname, so it'll work automatically for the
  Fahrzeuge dropdown and any pages we add next. Want me to move on to building
  the Fahrzeuge pages?

✻ Churned for 2m 6s

❯ fix this [Image #108]                                                         
  ⎿  [Image #108]

⏺ That's a horizontal-overflow artifact — the closed mobile menu panel sits
  off-screen to the right (translateX(100%)), which extends the document width
  and shows as the cream strip on the right (a full-page screenshot captures
  that extra width). The panel lives in a fixed overlay, which body { 
  overflow-x: clip } can't contain. Let me clip it at the overlay itself:

⏺ Update(src/components/Nav.astro)
  ⎿  Added 2 lines
      217      position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 200;
      218      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);
      219      opacity: 0; visibility: hidden;
      220 +    /* Contain the off-screen panel so it can't widen the docu
          +ment. */                                                      
      221 +    overflow: hidden;                                         
      222      transition: opacity 0.35s ease, visibility 0s linear 0.35s
           ;
      223    }
      224    .nav__overlay[aria-hidden='false'] { opacity: 1; visibility:
            visible; transition: opacity 0.35s ease; }

  Ran 1 shell command (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ Fixed (build clean). The only off-screen-right transform is the nav panel, and
  it's now contained.

  The cause: the mobile menu, when closed, parks its panel off-screen to the
  right with transform: translateX(100%). Because it lives in a position: fixed
  overlay, the overflow-x: clip on <body> couldn't rein it in (fixed elements
  escape ancestor overflow clipping) — so the panel's off-screen width extended
  the document, which a full-page screenshot renders as that cream strip beside
  the hero.

  The fix: added overflow: hidden to the .nav__overlay itself (the fixed
  container), so the parked panel is clipped within the viewport and no longer
  widens the page. The slide-in animation still works exactly the same — the
  panel is just clipped while it's off-screen.

⏺ Both guards are in and building clean. There are now three layers stopping
  horizontal scroll:

  1. .nav__overlay { overflow: hidden } — clips the off-screen menu panel at its
  own fixed container (the real culprit — fixed elements are positioned against
  the viewport, so body's clip can't reach them).
  2. html { overflow-x: clip } — root-level guard against any other
  fixed/absolute element ever widening the document.
  3. body { overflow-x: clip } — the existing net for normal-flow content.

  The symptom in both #108 and #109 was the same: the parked menu panel added
  ~340px of off-screen width, so the page could scroll sideways (#109 = scrolled
  right, content cut off left; #108 = the extra width shown as the cream
  strip).

  ⎿  Added 49 lines
      101    <Footer />
      102  </Layout>
      103  
      104 +<script define:vars={{ mailto: CONTACT.email.mailto }}>       
      105 +  // Contact form handling.                                   
      106 +  // 1) Set FORM_ENDPOINT to your form service (Formspree/Web3
          +Forms/eigenes Backend)                                        
      107 +  //    to submit via fetch. 2) If left empty, the form opens 
          +a pre-filled E-Mail.                                          
      108 +  const FORM_ENDPOINT = '';                                   
      109 +                                                              
      110 +  const form = document.querySelector('[data-contact-form]'); 
      111 +  const status = form?.querySelector('[data-form-status]');   
      112 +  form?.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {             
      113 +    e.preventDefault();                                       
      114 +    if (!form.reportValidity()) return;                       
      115 +    const btn = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');  
      116 +    const data = new FormData(form);                          
      117 +                                                              
      118 +    if (FORM_ENDPOINT) {                                      
      119 +      btn.disabled = true;                                    
      120 +      status.className = 'kform__status';                     
      121 +      status.textContent = 'Wird gesendet …';                 
      122 +      try {                                                   
      123 +        const res = await fetch(FORM_ENDPOINT, { method: 'POST
          +', body: data, headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } });    
      124 +        if (!res.ok) throw new Error();                       
      125 +        form.reset();                                         
      126 +        status.className = 'kform__status is-ok';             
      127 +        status.textContent = 'Vielen Dank! Wir haben Ihre Anfr
          +age erhalten und melden uns umgehend.';                       
      128 +      } catch {                                               
      129 +        status.className = 'kform__status is-err';            
      130 +        status.textContent = 'Es ist ein Fehler aufgetreten. B
          +itte rufen Sie uns an oder schreiben Sie eine E-Mail.';       
      131 +      } finally {                                             
      132 +        btn.disabled = false;                                 
      133 +      }                                                       
      134 +      return;                                                 
      135 +    }                                                         
      136 +                                                              
      137 +    // Fallback: compose a pre-filled E-Mail.                 
      138 +    const subject = `Anfrage: ${data.get('leistung') || 'Allge
          +mein'}`;                                                      
      139 +    const body = [                                            
      140 +      `Name: ${data.get('name') || ''}`,                      
      141 +      `E-Mail: ${data.get('email') || ''}`,                   
      142 +      `Telefon: ${data.get('telefon') || ''}`,                
      143 +      `Leistung: ${data.get('leistung') || ''}`,              
      144 +      '',                                                     
      145 +      `${data.get('nachricht') || ''}`,                       
      146 +    ].join('\n');                                             
      147 +    window.location.href = `${mailto}?subject=${encodeURICompo
          +nent(subject)}&body=${encodeURIComponent(body)}`;             
      148 +    status.className = 'kform__status is-ok';                 
      149 +    status.textContent = 'Ihr E-Mail-Programm wurde geöffnet. 
          +Alternativ erreichen Sie uns telefonisch.';                   
      150 +  });                                                         
      151 +</script>                                                     
      152 +                                                              
      153  <style>
      154    .kontakt { background: var(--color-cream); color: #0c0a08; p
           adding-block: clamp(2.5rem, 5vw, 4rem) var(--section-py); }
      155    .kontakt__head { max-inline-size: 52ch; }

⏺ Done — real contact form is live (build clean).

  Kontakt form

  Replaced the placeholder with a proper form: Name*, Telefon, E-Mail*, Leistung
  (dropdown of all services), Nachricht*, and a Datenschutz consent checkbox*.
  It has HTML5 validation, focus/error states, brand-styled inputs (cream
  fields, terracotta focus ring), and is responsive (2-col → 1-col on mobile).

  How submission works right now:
  - Out of the box it works via a pre-filled email — on submit it opens the
  visitor's mail client addressed to your office email with all fields filled
  in. No backend needed.
  - To make it submit silently to an inbox, set one line — FORM_ENDPOINT in the
  form's <script> — to your form service URL (Formspree / Web3Forms / your own
  backend). Then it POSTs via fetch and shows an inline "Vielen Dank!" success
  message instead. If the client's "Form Setup" doc uses a specific service,
  that URL goes right there.

  The booking page — where it is

  It's at /online-buchung. You reach it today via the "Preis berechnen" button

⏺ Both done — build clean, 23 pages now.

  Fahrzeuge overview — rebuilt

  - One clean grid of all 6 vehicles (no more fragmented per-class sections).
  - Each card now carries its class as a frosted badge (top-left) — so the
  category lives inside the card, as you suggested.
  - Nicer card treatment: taller 4:5 tiles, refined gradient, big name + spec
  line, and an arrow button that fills terracotta + nudges on hover; image zooms
  subtly on hover.

  Preise — why a page beats a dropdown

  A "Preise" dropdown linking to each vehicle would just mirror the Fahrzeuge 
  dropdown (same list, same targets) — redundant. So I built a dedicated /preise
  page where all rates sit side-by-side for actual comparison:
  - An "inklusive" strip (Wartezeit, Maut & Parkgebühren, Fixpreis, USt).
  - A pricing card per vehicle — class badge + name + its three rates
  (Flughafentransfer / pro Stunde / pro Tag) + a "Fahrzeug ansehen" link.
  - A note about Richtwerte + custom quotes, then the CTA band.
  - Nav "Preise" now points to /preise (footer too) with a working active state
  — no more homepage-anchor.

  This gives people a real at-a-glance price comparison, and each card still
  routes to the full vehicle page. Reload /fahrzeuge and /preise to see them.
  Want the Preise cards as a comparison table instead (vehicles as rows, rate
  types as columns) — or is the card layout good?

⏺ Done — build clean. "Buchen" removed from the nav; booking is now reached via
  buttons only.

  How I split the CTAs

  Intent: Booking — "I want a ride now"
  Buttons → /online-buchung (Booking): Homepage hero "Jetzt buchen" · each
    vehicle-page hero "Jetzt buchen" · all pricing-card buttons (Transfer / 
    Stunden /  Tag buchen) · homepage booking bar (Preis  berechnen)
  Buttons → /kontakt (Contact): —
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Intent: Contact — "I have a question / custom request"
  Buttons → /online-buchung (Booking): —
  Buttons → /kontakt (Contact): The CTA band (Jetzt anfragen) · FAQ ·
    service-page CTAs (hero, intro, Innenraum) · the vehicle Innenraum CTA · nav

    & footer "Kontakt"
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Intent: Phone
  Buttons → /online-buchung (Booking): — (both keep tel:)
  Buttons → /kontakt (Contact): —

  The logic: the high-intent, decision-made moments — the hero and the pricing —
  go straight to the booking system. The softer "ask us / tailor it" moments —
  the closing CTA band, FAQ, and the service pages (where someone's still
  exploring) — go to the contact form.

  I also relabeled the booking-bound buttons so the wording matches the
  destination: hero buttons now say "Jetzt buchen", and the pricing buttons are
  "… buchen" (were "… anfragen"). The inquiry buttons keep "Jetzt anfragen".

  Net result: Book = hero + prices; Ask = everything else. If you'd rather the
  homepage/vehicle heroes stay "anfragen" → contact and only prices drive
  booking, that's a one-line flip — just say so.

      157 +<script define:vars={{ mailto: CONTACT.email.mailto }}>       
      158 +  // Booking form. Set FORM_ENDPOINT (Formspree/Web3Forms/eige
          +nes Backend) to submit                                        
      159 +  // via fetch and redirect to /buchung-abschluss. Empty = pre
          +-filled E-Mail fallback.                                      
      160 +  const FORM_ENDPOINT = '';                                   
      161 +                                                              
      162 +  const form = document.querySelector('[data-booking-form]'); 
      163 +  if (form) {                                                 
      164 +    const status = form.querySelector('[data-form-status]');  
      165 +    const ziel = form.querySelector('[data-ziel]');           
      166 +    const dauer = form.querySelector('[data-dauer]');         
      167 +    const zielInput = ziel?.querySelector('input');           
      168 +                                                              
      169 +    // Toggle Zieladresse vs. Dauer based on Fahrttyp.        
      170 +    form.querySelectorAll('input[name="fahrttyp"]').forEach((r
          +) =>                                                          
      171 +      r.addEventListener('change', () => {                    
      172 +        const hourly = form.querySelector('input[name="fahrtty
          +p"]:checked')?.value === 'Stundenbuchung';                    
      173 +        ziel.hidden = hourly;                                 
      174 +        if (zielInput) zielInput.required = !hourly;          
      175 +        dauer.hidden = !hourly;                               
      176 +      })                                                      
      177 +    );                                                        
      178 +                                                              
      179 +    // Default date = today.                                  
      180 +    const d = form.querySelector('input[name="datum"]');      
      181 +    if (d && !d.value) { const t = new Date(); d.min = t.toISO
          +String().slice(0, 10); }                                      
      182 +                                                              
      183 +    form.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {            
      184 +      e.preventDefault();                                     
      185 +      if (!form.reportValidity()) return;                     
      186 +      const btn = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
      187 +      const data = new FormData(form);                        
      188 +                                                              
      189 +      if (FORM_ENDPOINT) {                                    
      190 +        btn.disabled = true;                                  
      191 +        status.className = 'bform__status';                   
      192 +        status.textContent = 'Wird gesendet …';               
      193 +        try {                                                 
      194 +          const res = await fetch(FORM_ENDPOINT, { method: 'PO
          +ST', body: data, headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } });  
      195 +          if (!res.ok) throw new Error();                     
      196 +          window.location.href = '/buchung-abschluss';        
      197 +        } catch {                                             
      198 +          btn.disabled = false;                               
      199 +          status.className = 'bform__status is-err';          
      200 +          status.textContent = 'Es ist ein Fehler aufgetreten.
          + Bitte rufen Sie uns an.';                                    
      201 +        }                                                     
      202 +        return;                                               
      203 +      }                                                       
      204 +                                                              
      205 +      // Fallback: pre-filled E-Mail.                         
      206 +      const g = (k) => data.get(k) || '';                     
      207 +      const subject = `Buchungsanfrage: ${g('fahrzeug')} — ${g
          +('datum')} ${g('uhrzeit')}`;                                  
      208 +      const body = [                                          
      209 +        `Fahrttyp: ${g('fahrttyp')}`,                         
      210 +        `Abholadresse: ${g('abholadresse')}`,                 
      211 +        g('fahrttyp') === 'Stundenbuchung' ? `Dauer: ${g('daue
          +r')}` : `Zieladresse: ${g('zieladresse')}`,                   
      212 +        `Datum / Uhrzeit: ${g('datum')} um ${g('uhrzeit')}`,  
      213 +        `Personen: ${g('personen')} · Gepäck: ${g('gepaeck')}`
          +,                                                             
      214 +        `Fahrzeug: ${g('fahrzeug')}`,                         
      215 +        '',                                                   
      216 +        `Name: ${g('name')}`,                                 
      217 +        `Telefon: ${g('telefon')}`,                           
      218 +        `E-Mail: ${g('email')}`,                              
      219 +        `Anmerkungen: ${g('anmerkungen')}`,                   
      220 +      ].join('\n');                                           
      221 +      window.location.href = `${mailto}?subject=${encodeURICom
          +ponent(subject)}&body=${encodeURIComponent(body)}`;           
      222 +      status.className = 'bform__status is-ok';               
      223 +      status.textContent = 'Ihr E-Mail-Programm wurde geöffnet
          +. Alternativ erreichen Sie uns telefonisch.';                 
      224 +    });                                                       
      225 +  }                                                           
      226 +</script>                                                     
      227 +

⏺ Both done — build clean.

  Booking system (/online-buchung)

  Replaced the placeholder with a full, working booking form:
  - Step 1 · Ihre Fahrt — Fahrttyp (Einzelfahrt / Hin- & Rückfahrt /
  Stundenbuchung — which swaps the Zieladresse field for a Dauer selector),
  Abhol-/Zieladresse, Datum, Uhrzeit, Personen, Gepäck. Date defaults to today+.
  - Step 2 · Fahrzeug wählen — selectable vehicle cards (photo + class + "ab €…"
  pulled live from the fleet data), with a checkmark on the chosen one.
  - Step 3 · Kontaktdaten — Name, Telefon, E-Mail, Anmerkungen, Datenschutz
  consent.
  - Submit builds a complete booking summary and: with a FORM_ENDPOINT set →
  POSTs and redirects to /buchung-abschluss (the thank-you page); without one →
  opens a pre-filled email (works today). Plus a sticky sidebar (24/7 +
  benefits) and full responsiveness.

  ▎ It's a booking request system on a static site. Live price calculation / 
  ▎ payment would need your booking service or a backend — the form is built to 
  ▎ POST straight to it (one line: FORM_ENDPOINT), or you can drop a third-party
  ▎ widget into that same slot.

  Non-clicking buttons — fixed

  The service cards ("Jetzt anfragen") were the bug. The carousel's track called
  setPointerCapture on every pointer-down, which hijacked the pointer so the
  card link never received its click. The drag doesn't need capture (its move/up
  listeners are on window), so I now capture only on the dial (the drag
  handle). Card links click through again, and dragging still works.

  Audited the rest of the site: no other setPointerCapture anywhere, and the
  FleetShowcase's overlays all use pointer-events: none with the interactive
  layer above them — so its tabs, play button, and "Fahrzeug ansehen" all work.
  Reload the homepage and click a service card to confirm.

⏺ Done — build clean, curtain's on every page.

  Preloader (first visit)

  A dark full-screen intro: the logo fades + scales in while a terracotta 
  progress line fills beneath it, then the whole panel slides up to reveal the
  page. It holds for ~1.1s (waits for content, capped) so it reads as a brand
  moment, not a stall.

  Page transition (every internal click)

  The curtain slides down to cover the screen, the page navigates behind it (no
  white flash), and the new page's curtain slides back up — a smooth wipe
  between pages. Snappier after the first load (~0.3s reveal vs the ~1.1s
  preloader, tracked per session).

  Built to be safe

  - Doesn't touch any component — lives entirely in the Layout, so the carousel,
  forms, booking system, nav, etc. are untouched (each page still fully
  reloads, so all scripts re-run normally).
  - Respects prefers-reduced-motion (curtain disabled, normal navigation).
  - No-JS fallback (curtain hidden so the page is never blocked).
  - Back/forward cache handled (no stuck curtain on back-button).
  - Only intercepts internal, same-tab, left-click links — skips #anchors,
  tel:/mailto:, external, new-tab, and cmd/ctrl-clicks — and yields if another
  handler already claimed the click (so it won't fight the carousel fix).

  Reload the site and click around to feel it. Easy knobs if you want to tune:
  preloader duration, wipe speed/direction (currently slides up), and the
  preloader content (logo + line — could swap for a wordmark reveal or add a %
  counter). Want any of those adjusted?
